# 2019–2026 Concentration Analysis

## Methodology

- Source: every canonical `**Primary route:**` attached to an explicit paper anchor, exported to `CORPUS-METADATA.json`.
- **Answerable unit** excludes structural parent anchors in qualifying-language papers and the six GS-IV case parents. Each GS-IV case parent carries its marks once; its separately routed demands carry unit counts but zero additional marks.
- Marks are **represented marks**, so all solved alternatives are counted: qualifying Hindi and English each represent 600 marks per year, Essay 1,000, each Philosophy paper 400, while the candidate-attempt totals remain 300, 250 and 250 respectively.
- Prelims/CSAT concentration uses printed question counts, including officially dropped items as solved learning units. Scoring exceptions are listed under caveats.
- Secondary routes are excluded from every primary total to prevent double counting.

## Corpus reconciliation

- Paper files: **88 = 8 years × 11 paper families**.
- Routed stable anchors: **3,600**.
- Answerable solved units: **3,416**.
- Canonical topic pages: **353**.
- Represented marks across Mains/Philosophy: **32,000**.

| 2019 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; no item was dropped. |
| 2020 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Set-A keys are official-final; GS-I Q27 and Q52 were dropped. |
| 2021 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; GS-I Q80 was dropped and CSAT Q39 accepted C or D. |
| 2022 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; GS-I Q61 and CSAT Q36 were dropped. |
| 2023 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; GS-I Q34 was dropped. |
| 2024 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; GS-I Q20, Q52 and Q57 were dropped. |
| 2025 | 100 | 80 | Both Prelims Series-A keys are official-final; no released scoring exception remains. |
| 2026 | 100 | 80 | Both repository-held Prelims keys remain provisional; GS-I Q64 is dropped in the provisional key. |

## Explicit caveats

- The 2026 held keys remain provisional and are never promoted to official-final.
- Official drops and the 2021 CSAT multiple-answer outcome remain printed questions and solved learning units, but their scoring status is separately preserved.
- Represented marks measure the released library, not what a candidate writes in one sitting.
- Parent anchors improve navigation and case-level marks accounting; they are not answerable-unit inflation.
- Canonical routing uses exactly one primary topic. Secondary links are optional, justified and excluded from concentration totals.
